**1 · Summary · carries a judgement**

## How it affects you

| | Row | In short |
|---|---|---|
| 🟧 | Cost | adds 14,854 characters and bytes to every session, ~3,714 tokens (10,006 characters of skill descriptions, 754 characters of agent descriptions, 4,094 bytes of rule files, 0 hook text) (not read in full) |
| 🟨 | Reach | a rule file in every main session (not read in full) |
| 🟧 | Leaves state | 22 files that may write state |
| 🟨 | Live surface | no hook commands, MCP servers or mods found (not read in full) |
| 🟨 | Read completeness | partly read |

- **Leaves state**: the files are listed in the "State it may leave" card under [What it adds](#what-it-adds).
- **Read completeness**: not read in full: hooks (partial), marketplace (partial), persistence_candidates (partial).

<details><summary><b>How the colours are set</b></summary>

The colours follow written rules. Each is checked top to bottom, and the first match wins. A row whose input was not read in full is never 🟩.

- Cost: 🟩 under 2,000; 🟨 2,000 to 7,999; 🟧 8,000 to 31,999; 🟥 32,000 and over. Size counts skill and agent descriptions in characters, and rule files and hook-injected text in bytes (the dossier's own units), so a total that holds both says "characters and bytes"; text a hook builds at run time counts as at least its known size. Hook text counts twice when hooks reach subagents. An entry that was refused or has no usable size counts as 0.
- Reach: 🟥 a hook and hooks reach subagents; 🟨 a hook, a Claude Code mod or a rule file (every main session); 🟩 neither (on demand only).
- Leaves state: 🟧 one or more files that may write state; 🟨 nothing found but the scan was partial or failed; 🟩 nothing found by a full scan.
- Live surface: 🟥 a hook or MCP command runs ssh, mosh, telnet, rsh, rlogin (each a remote shell); 🟧 any MCP server, any Claude Code mod, a hook running curl, wget, nc or holding an http:// or https:// URL, or a hook command too long to read; 🟨 any hook command; 🟩 none. A command word is the first word of each piece, cut at newlines and at | && || ; & outside quotes, after NAME=value words, by basename; an MCP server's command is compared by its first word and each of its args whole, by basename. The scan does not search inline code (bash -c, node -e), hook script bodies, or commands behind a wrapper (sudo, timeout, command, nohup, exec, xargs, ( ), { }, !, $( ) or backticks).
- Read completeness: 🟩 read in full; 🟨 partly read; 🟥 something unread.

</details>

[`skills/constraint-driven-development/references/floor-guard.md:11`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/constraint-driven-development/references/floor-guard.md#L11)
- "Commit the completed task only when the user or repository workflow authorizes it." [`skills/context-engineering/SKILL.md:133`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/context-engineering/SKILL.md#L133)
- "Otherwise, leave the working tree intact and record that the changes are uncommitted." [`skills/context-engineering/SKILL.md:133`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/context-engineering/SKILL.md#L133)
- "In the fresh session, read the rules, spec, plan, task status, and actual \`git status\` before acting." [`skills/context-engineering/SKILL.md:135`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/context-engineering/SKILL.md#L135)
- "No commits in 6+ months but active consumers exist" [`skills/deprecation-and-migration/SKILL.md:201`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/deprecation-and-migration/SKILL.md#L201)
- "Never rewrite or collapse earlier entries: git keeps the old text, but the log is the only record of the reasons." [`skills/documentation-and-adrs/SKILL.md:283`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/documentation-and-adrs/SKILL.md#L283)
- ""I'll tidy the change log while I'm in here" | Git holds the old text; the log holds the reasons. Rewriting it destroys the only copy of the reasoning and preserves nothing." [`skills/documentation-and-adrs/SKILL.md:294`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/documentation-and-adrs/SKILL.md#L294)
- ""Spawning a reviewer is expensive" | Debugging a wrong commit in production is more expensive. The check is bounded; the bug isn't." [`skills/doubt-driven-development/SKILL.md:200`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/doubt-driven-development/SKILL.md#L200)
- "Treat commits as save points, branches as sandboxes, and history as documentation." [`skills/git-workflow-and-versioning/SKILL.md:10`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/git-workflow-and-versioning/SKILL.md#L10)
- "Don't accumulate large uncommitted changes." [`skills/git-workflow-and-versioning/SKILL.md:36`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/git-workflow-and-versioning/SKILL.md#L36)
- "Commit messages explain the *why*, not just the *what*:" [`skills/git-workflow-and-versioning/SKILL.md:67`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/git-workflow-and-versioning/SKILL.md#L67)
- "Each type of change should be a separate commit — and ideally a separate PR:" [`skills/git-workflow-and-versioning/SKILL.md:98`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/git-workflow-and-versioning/SKILL.md#L98)
- "**Commit generated files** only if the project expects them (e.g., \`package-lock.json\`, Prisma migrations)" [`skills/git-workflow-and-versioning/SKILL.md:246`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/git-workflow-and-versioning/SKILL.md#L246)
- "**Don't commit** build output (\`dist/\`, \`.next/\`), environment files (\`.env\`), or IDE config (\`.vscode/settings.json\` unless shared)" [`skills/git-workflow-and-versioning/SKILL.md:247`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/git-workflow-and-versioning/SKILL.md#L247)
- ""I'll commit when the feature is done" | One giant commit is impossible to review, debug, or revert. Commit each slice." [`skills/git-workflow-and-versioning/SKILL.md:317`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/git-workflow-and-versioning/SKILL.md#L317)
- ""I'll squash it all later" | Squashing destroys the development narrative. Prefer clean incremental commits from the start." [`skills/git-workflow-and-versioning/SKILL.md:319`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/git-workflow-and-versioning/SKILL.md#L319)

- "Don't defer just because it's "fresh."" [`skills/doubt-driven-development/SKILL.md:181`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/doubt-driven-development/SKILL.md#L181)
- "Stop when:" [`skills/doubt-driven-development/SKILL.md:185`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/doubt-driven-development/SKILL.md#L185)
- "3 cycles completed (escalate to user, don't grind a fourth alone), **or**" [`skills/doubt-driven-development/SKILL.md:188`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/doubt-driven-development/SKILL.md#L188)
- "Surface this to the user — three unresolved cycles is information about the artifact, not a reason to keep looping." [`skills/doubt-driven-development/SKILL.md:191`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/doubt-driven-development/SKILL.md#L191)
- "Do not lift the bound." [`skills/doubt-driven-development/SKILL.md:193`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/doubt-driven-development/SKILL.md#L193)
- ""I'm confident, skip the doubt step" | Confidence correlates poorly with correctness on novel problems. Moments of certainty are exactly when blind spots hide." [`skills/doubt-driven-development/SKILL.md:199`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/doubt-driven-development/SKILL.md#L199)

- "**Prefer composition over configuration:**" [`skills/frontend-ui-engineering/SKILL.md:42`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/frontend-ui-engineering/SKILL.md#L42)
- "**Keep components focused:**" [`skills/frontend-ui-engineering/SKILL.md:64`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/frontend-ui-engineering/SKILL.md#L64)
- "When the product needs a distinct visual direction, collect evidence before choosing a layout:" [`skills/frontend-ui-engineering/SKILL.md:124`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/frontend-ui-engineering/SKILL.md#L124)
- "Search a trusted reference catalogue or use references supplied by the product team." [`skills/frontend-ui-engineering/SKILL.md:126`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/frontend-ui-engineering/SKILL.md#L126)

- "Shadow-heavy design | Layered shadows add depth that competes with content and slows rendering on low-end devices | Subtle or no shadows unless the design system specifies" [`skills/frontend-ui-engineering/SKILL.md:146`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/frontend-ui-engineering/SKILL.md#L146)
- "Use a consistent spacing scale." [`skills/frontend-ui-engineering/SKILL.md:150`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/frontend-ui-engineering/SKILL.md#L150)
- "Don't use heading styles for non-heading content." [`skills/frontend-ui-engineering/SKILL.md:172`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/frontend-ui-engineering/SKILL.md#L172)

- "**Eliminate:** What happens if you remove a feature entirely? What's the version with zero configuration? What would it look like with half the steps?" [`skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md:14`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md#L14)
- "Start with an observation or pain point" [`skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md:23`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md#L23)
- "Narrow enough to be actionable ("...help new users find relevant content in their first 5 minutes")" [`skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md:28`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md#L28)
- "Broad enough to allow creative solutions (not "...add a recommendation sidebar")" [`skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md:29`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md#L29)
- "**What are we assuming?** List every assumption, even the ones that feel obvious" [`skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md:44`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md#L44)
- "**Rebuild from the truths.** If you only had the fundamental truths, what would you build?" [`skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md:46`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md#L46)
- "**Key insight:** People don't buy products — they hire them to do a job." [`skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md:60`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md#L60)
- "The competing product isn't always in the same category. (Netflix competes with sleep, not just other streaming services.)" [`skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md:60`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md#L60)
- "**Time constraint:** "What if you only had 1 day to build this?"" [`skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md:68`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md#L68)
- "**Feature constraint:** "What if it could only have one feature?"" [`skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md:69`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md#L69)
- "**Tech constraint:** "What if you couldn't use \[the obvious technology\]?"" [`skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md:70`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md#L70)
- "**Audience constraint:** "What if your user had never used a computer before?"" [`skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md:72`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md#L72)
- "List every plausible reason for failure — technical, market, team, timing" [`skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md:82`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md#L82)
- "Look at how other domains solved similar problems:" [`skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md:90`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md#L90)
- "What would this look like if \[specific company/product\] built it?" [`skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md:93`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/frameworks.md#L93)
- "Not every criterion applies to every idea — use judgment about which dimensions matter most for the specific context." [`skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md:3`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md#L3)
- "Can you name 3 specific people who have this problem right now?" [`skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md:16`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md#L16)
- "What are they doing today instead? (The real competitor is always the current workaround.)" [`skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md:17`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md#L17)
- ""Everyone could use this" — if you can't name a specific user, the value isn't clear" [`skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md:23`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md#L23)
- "Can you actually build this?" [`skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md:29`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md#L29)
- "What's the minimum team/effort to build an MVP?" [`skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md:38`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md#L38)
- "Does it require specialized expertise you don't have?" [`skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md:39`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md#L39)
- ""We just need to solve \[very hard research problem\] first"" [`skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md:48`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md#L48)
- "What's the one thing this does that nothing else does? (If you can't name one, that's a problem.)" [`skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md:58`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md#L58)
- "Is the difference something users actually care about, or just something builders find interesting?" [`skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md:60`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md#L60)
- "For every idea direction, explicitly list assumptions in three categories:" [`skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md:77`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md#L77)
- "Assumptions that significantly impact success but don't kill the idea." [`skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md:85`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md#L85)
- "Example: "Users prefer self-serve over talking to a person" — if wrong, you need a different go-to-market, but the core product can still work." [`skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md:87`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md#L87)
- "Don't validate these until the core is proven." [`skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md:90`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md#L90)
- "**Low Value** | Only if trivial | Don't do this" [`skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md:101`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md#L101)
- "**One job, done well.** The MVP should nail exactly one user job. Not three jobs done partially." [`skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md:109`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md#L109)
- "**If it's not embarrassing, you waited too long.** The first version should feel incomplete to the builder. If it doesn't, you over-built." [`skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md:113`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/idea-refine/refinement-criteria.md#L113)
- "Avoid implementing an entire feature in one pass." [`skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md:10`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md#L10)
- "**Move to the next slice** — carry forward, don't restart" [`skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md:42`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md#L42)
- "When backend and frontend need to develop in parallel:" [`skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md:68`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md#L68)
- "Do NOT:" [`skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md:119`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md#L119) (also in `skills/using-agent-skills/SKILL.md:101`)
- "Add features not in the spec because they "seem useful"" [`skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md:123`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md#L123) (also in `skills/using-agent-skills/SKILL.md:106`)
- "Don't mix concerns:" [`skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md:137`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md#L137)
- "If a feature isn't ready for users but you need to merge increments:" [`skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md:149`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md#L149)
- "New code should default to safe, conservative behavior:" [`skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md:164`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md#L164)
- "Each increment should be independently revertable:" [`skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md:176`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md#L176)
- "Database migrations should have corresponding rollback migrations" [`skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md:180`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md#L180)
- "\[ \] The build succeeds (the repository's build command)" [`skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md:205`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md#L205)
- "After a successful run, don't repeat the same command unless the code has changed since — re-running on unchanged code adds no information." [`skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md:211`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md#L211)
- ""Let me run the build command again just to be sure" | After a successful run, repeating the same command adds nothing unless the code has changed since. Run it again after subsequent edits, not as r…" [`skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md:222`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md#L222)
- "\[ \] The build is clean" [`skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md:243`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md#L243)
- "Per-increment verification is the local check." [`skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md:249`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/incremental-implementation/SKILL.md#L249)
- "The cheapest moment to find this gap is before any plan, spec, or code exists." [`skills/interview-me/SKILL.md:12`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/interview-me/SKILL.md#L12)
- "The request is conventional rather than specific ("build me X", "make it faster") and you can't unpack the convention without guessing" [`skills/interview-me/SKILL.md:21`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/interview-me/SKILL.md#L21)
- "You're tempted to start with assumptions you haven't surfaced" [`skills/interview-me/SKILL.md:22`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/interview-me/SKILL.md#L22)
- "This skill needs a live, responsive user. **Do not invoke in non-interactive contexts** like CI pipelines, scheduled runs, \`/loop\`, or autonomous-loop." [`skills/interview-me/SKILL.md:36`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/interview-me/SKILL.md#L36)
- "The user can't react to your hypotheses if you bury them in a list" [`skills/interview-me/SKILL.md:66`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/interview-me/SKILL.md#L66)
- "Phrases like "I should probably…", "I think I'm supposed to…", "good engineering practice says…"" [`skills/interview-me/SKILL.md:85`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/interview-me/SKILL.md#L85)
- "When your confidence is high, write back what you now think the user wants." [`skills/interview-me/SKILL.md:96`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/interview-me/SKILL.md#L96)
- "Stop interviewing and produce the restate." [`skills/interview-me/SKILL.md:138`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/interview-me/SKILL.md#L138)
- "**\`spec-driven-development\`**: downstream. If the confirmed intent is concrete ("I want X for Y users with Z success criteria"), hand off to \`spec-driven-development\` to write it down." [`skills/interview-me/SKILL.md:191`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/interview-me/SKILL.md#L191)
- "**\`source-driven-development\`**: orthogonal. Interview-me clarifies what the user wants; SDD verifies framework facts. They don't compete." [`skills/interview-me/SKILL.md:194`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/interview-me/SKILL.md#L194)
- "A confidence number below ~70% with no reason attached: the user can't help close the gap if they don't know what's missing" [`skills/interview-me/SKILL.md:219`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/interview-me/SKILL.md#L219)
- "Saving the intent doc before the user has confirmed (the doc itself implies a yes the user didn't give)" [`skills/interview-me/SKILL.md:220`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/interview-me/SKILL.md#L220)
- "Building any feature that will run in production" [`skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md:14`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md#L14)
- "Diagnosing a failure happening right now — use the \`debugging-and-error-recovery\` skill (observability is what makes that skill fast next time)" [`skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md:21`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md#L21)
- "Profiling and optimizing measured slowness — use the \`performance-optimization\` skill" [`skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md:22`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md#L22)
- "If you can't name the questions, you're not ready to instrument — you'll log everything and learn nothing." [`skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md:40`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md#L40)
- "\`error\` | Invariant broken; someone may need to act | Investigate" [`skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md:74`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md#L74)
- "Stamp the entry point where the run starts, next to the correlation ID, and propagate both the same way:" [`skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md:91`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md#L91)
- "Allowlist fields; don't log whole request bodies." [`skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md:106`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md#L106)
- "For resources (queues, pools, hosts), use **USE**: **U**tilization, **S**aturation, **E**rrors." [`skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md:110`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md#L110)
- "The example below uses Prometheus' \`prom-client\` — one common backend choice, not the only one; the RED/USE and cardinality rules are identical either way." [`skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md:112`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md#L112)
- "Labels must come from small, fixed sets (route template, status class, provider name)." [`skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md:125`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md#L125)
- "Track averages never, percentiles always: an average hides the 1% of users having a terrible time." [`skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md:132`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md#L132)
- "Use histograms and read p50/p95/p99." [`skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md:132`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md#L132)
- "Add manual spans only around meaningful internal units of work (e.g., \`applyDiscounts\`, \`chargeProvider\`) and attach the attributes on-call will filter by." [`skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md:150`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md#L150)
- "Sample head-based at a low rate by default; keep 100% of errors if your backend supports tail sampling." [`skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md:150`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md#L150)
- "Use two severities only: **page** (user-facing, act now) and **ticket** (degradation, act this week). A third tier becomes noise that trains people to ignore everything." [`skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md:170`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md#L170)
- "Follow one request across services in the tracing UI → no broken spans" [`skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md:196`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md#L196)
- ""I'll add logging after it works" | "After" becomes "after the first incident", which is the most expensive moment to discover you're blind. Instrument as you build." [`skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md:203`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md#L203)
- ""Alert on everything important, we'll tune later" | A noisy pager trains people to ignore it. The tuning never happens; the missed real page does." [`skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md:207`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md#L207)
- "Latency tracked as an average with no percentiles" [`skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md:218`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md#L218)
- ""It works on my machine" as the only evidence a production feature is healthy" [`skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md:222`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md#L222)
- "\[ \] Every log sink written by more than one entry point carries an entry-point field, set where the run starts and propagated with the correlation ID rather than inferred downstream" [`skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md:230`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md#L230)
- "For the at-a-glance version of this list, including the pre-launch instrumentation gate, see \`../../references/observability-checklist.md\`." [`skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md:238`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/observability-and-instrumentation/SKILL.md#L238)
- "Optimize only what measurements prove matters." [`skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md:10`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md#L10)
- "**When NOT to use:** Don't optimize before you have evidence of a problem." [`skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md:20`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md#L20)
- "Two complementary approaches — use both:" [`skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md:42`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md#L42)
- "Use the symptom to decide what to measure first:" [`skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md:75`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md#L75)
- "High CLS | Images without dimensions, late-loading content, font shifts | Check layout shift attribution" [`skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md:108`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md#L108)
- "Slow API responses | N+1 queries, missing indexes, unoptimized queries | Check database query log" [`skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md:116`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md#L116)
- "**Unbounded data fetching.** Every list endpoint paginates with a limit and a stable order. \[Pattern\](references/optimization-patterns.md#unbounded-data-fetching)." [`skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md:128`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md#L128)
- "**Re-measure the way you measured the baseline:** same command, same conditions, same fixed budget (wall-clock, sample count, or request count)." [`skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md:143`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md#L143)
- "If they must ship together, measure each in isolation first." [`skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md:145`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md#L145)
- "Code you keep, you maintain forever." [`skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md:158`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md#L158)
- "Virtualize the list | INP 240ms → 90ms | kept | Long tasks gone from the trace." [`skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md:169`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md#L169)
- "Use the same LCP, INP, p95 latency, or other primary metric that justified the fix." [`skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md:176`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md#L176)
- "Use two complementary layers when the surface is user-facing:" [`skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md:179`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md#L179)
- "**Synthetic CI gate:** Catch reproducible regressions before merge with a performance budget. Repeat noisy measurements or compare a median/trend so normal run-to-run variance does not turn the gate …" [`skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md:181`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md#L181)
- "**Field monitoring:** Alert on a meaningful p75 movement in RUM data. Use attributed \`web-vitals\` data to locate the cause; treat CrUX's rolling window as confirmation rather than an immediate alert." [`skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md:184`](https://github.com/addyosmani/agent-skills/blob/39b723c/skills/performance-optimization/SKILL.md#L184)
